Listed below are all 3-1 or better decks from the four rounds of individual Standard play during Day One of the World **Magic** Cup.

*Note: Christopher Virula's deck was accidentally left off this list. We apologize for the error.* 

Jaoa Andrade 

World Magic Cup 2012, Standard
